Read file /workspace/measurement.md, lines 1-32
1: # Always-on LDO measurement contract
2: 
3: Numerical stimulus settings come from spec.json. Loads are 1/20/100 uA;
4: external capacitors are 10/100 nF. Edges take 2 us, initial hold is 100 us,
5: isolated plateaus are 200 us, and maximum sample gap is 50 ns. Burst plateaus
6: are 20 us with eight pulses and a 200 us tail. DC error and recovery use the
7: absolute 1.176–1.224 V band; maximum transient excursion is 96 mV from 1.2 V.
8: Recovery must complete within 100 us. Evaluate both load-edge directions.
9: Minimum-load DC overhead is at most 2 uA, other DC loads at most 10 uA.
10: Absolute VREF current is at most 1 uA. Use the signed terminal currents;
11: overhead = max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0). Preserve KCL within 1 nA.
12: No startup, protection, noise or yield requirement is included.
13: 
14: Each standalone DC, load profile, line profile or PSRR condition costs one native
15: case. A full nominal group has three DC cases and three load profiles (six cases).
16: Line transient and light/heavy-load PSRR at 1 kHz/1 MHz are reference measurements,
17: not scored. PSRR is 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)) at the regulated operating point.
18: Line stimulus goes from minimum to maximum VIN and back at heavy load.
19: 
20: Save full waveforms and OP currents. Settling is final entry into the fixed
21: recovery band that remains there through the observation end. Check initial
22: hold DC error and maximum post-edge excursion independently. Burst recovery is
23: checked after the final falling edge. A complete waveform that never recovers
24: has a null recovery plus its observation lower bound and fails if that bound
25: exceeds the limit. Truncated or invalid samples are measurement failures.
26: One submitted circuit must pass all required conditions without rounding.
27: 
28: ## Numerical precision
29: 
30: Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
31: Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
32: A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.

1: # LDO-ALWAYS-ON-SKY130
2: 
3: Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
4: Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
5: Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
6: Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
7: You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
8: No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
9: Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.
10: 
11: ## Time accounting
12: 
13: The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

1: # Shared client
2: 
3: Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
4: Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
5: `atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
6: `atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
7: `atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
8: Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
9: The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
10: Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
11: Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
12: Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.
13:

1: # Netlist grammar
2: 
3: Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4: Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5: MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6: Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7: SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8: ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9: R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10: Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11: Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.
12: 
13: Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:
14:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Local ngspice harness for the LDO design (free local iteration)."""
import os, re, subprocess, sys, tempfile

NG = "/workspace/work/inst/bin/ngspice"
if not os.path.exists(NG):
    NG = "/workspace/work/ng/src/ngspice"
MOD = "/workspace/work/sky130"
WORK = "/workspace/work"

CORNERS = {
    "N":   ("tt", 1.6, 27),
    "SLC": ("ss", 1.4, -20),
    "SLH": ("ss", 1.4, 85),
    "SHC": ("ss", 1.8, -20),
    "SHH": ("ss", 1.8, 85),
    "FLC": ("ff", 1.4, -20),
    "FLH": ("ff", 1.4, 85),
    "FHC": ("ff", 1.8, -20),
    "FHH": ("ff", 1.8, 85),
}

def models(corner):
    return "\n".join([
        f".include {MOD}/n_{corner[0]}.pm3.spice",
        f".include {MOD}/p_{corner[0]}.pm3.spice",
    ])

def read_dut(path, subname=None, ac=False):
    txt = open(path).read()
    return txt

def run(netlist, tag):
    os.makedirs(f"{WORK}/out", exist_ok=True)
    f = f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.sp"
    open(f, "w").write(netlist)
    p = subprocess.run([NG, "-b", "-o", f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.log", f],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=1200)
    log = open(f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.log").read() if os.path.exists(f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.log") else ""
    return p.returncode, log

def dc_netlist(dut, corner, iload, cl, subname="DUT", vbrk=False, acsrc=False):
    c = CORNERS[corner]
    return f"""* dc test
{models(c)}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {iload}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
X1 vdd vout 0 vref {subname}
{dut}
.control
op
set filetype=ascii
wrdata {WORK}/out/dc_{subname}_{corner}.dat v(vout) i(vdd) i(vref)
.endc
.end
"""

def tran_netlist(dut, corner, cl, profile, tstop, tstep="50n", subname="DUT"):
    """profile: list of (time, current) breakpoints"""
    c = CORNERS[corner]
    pwl = " ".join(f"{t} {i}" for t, i in profile)
    return f"""* tran test
{models(c)}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 pwl({pwl})
Cload vout 0 {cl}
X1 vdd vout 0 vref {subname}
{dut}
.control
tran {tstep} {tstop}
set filetype=ascii
wrdata {WORK}/out/tran_{subname}_{corner}.dat v(vout) i(vdd)
.endc
.end
"""

def ac_netlist(dutac, corner, iload, cl, subname="DUTA"):
    """Loop gain via voltage injection between the divider and the amp input."""
    c = CORNERS[corner]
    return f"""* ac loop gain test
{models(c)}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {iload}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
RF1 vout fbo 2e6
RF2 fbo 0 2e6
Vbrk fbi fbo dc 0 ac 1
X1 vdd vout 0 vref fbi {subname}
{dutac}
.control
ac dec 20 1 100meg
set filetype=ascii
wrdata {WORK}/out/ac_{corner}_{iload}.dat v(fbi) v(fbo)
.endc
.end
"""

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("harness module")

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys, os, re
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/work")
from ldotb import *

dut = open("/workspace/work/dut_v1.spice").read()
dutac = open("/workspace/work/dut_v1ac.spice").read()

def parse_dc(tag):
    f = f"{WORK}/out/dc_{tag}.dat"
    if not os.path.exists(f):
        return None
    rows = [l.split() for l in open(f) if l.strip() and not l.startswith("#")]
    # wrdata: v(vout) i(vdd) i(vref) -> 6 columns (x,y per vector)
    try:
        vout = float(rows[0][1]); ivdd = float(rows[0][3]); ivref = float(rows[0][5])
    except Exception:
        return None
    return vout, ivdd, ivref

def run_dc_all():
    print("corner  VOUT      Iin(uA)  Iref(uA)  overhead(uA)")
    for name in CORNERS:
        rc, log = run(dc_netlist(dut, name, "1u", "10n", "DUT"), f"dc1u_{name}")
        r = parse_dc(f"DUT_{name}")
        if r is None:
            print(name, "FAILED", [l for l in log.splitlines() if "rror" in l][:2])
            continue
        vout, ivdd, ivref = r
        iin = -ivdd
        print(f"{name:5s}  {vout:.6f}  {iin*1e6:8.3f}  {abs(ivref)*1e6:8.4f}  {(iin-1e-6)*1e6:8.3f}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_dc_all()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys, os, re, math
sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/work")
from ldotb import *

dut = open("/workspace/work/dut_v1.spice").read()
dutac = open("/workspace/work/dut_v1ac.spice").read()

def load_dat(f):
    t, y = [], []
    for l in open(f):
        if l.startswith("#") or not l.strip():
            continue
        p = l.split()
        t.append(float(p[0])); y.append(float(p[1]))
    return t, y

def tran_profile(iload_lo, iload_hi, cl, tag, corner="N", edge=2e-6, hold=100e-6, plat=200e-6, tail=200e-6):
    """light->heavy->light step profile"""
    t = [0, hold, hold+edge, hold+edge+plat, hold+edge+plat+edge, hold+edge+plat+edge+tail]
    i = [iload_lo, iload_lo, iload_hi, iload_hi, iload_lo, iload_lo]
    tstop = t[-1] + 10e-6
    prof = list(zip(t, i))
    net = tran_netlist(dut, corner, cl, prof, f"{tstop:.6g}", "50n", "DUT")
    rc, log = run(net, tag)
    f = f"{WORK}/out/tran_DUT_{corner}.dat"
    if not os.path.exists(f):
        print("TRAN FAIL", tag, log[-500:]); return None
    return load_dat(f), t

def analyze(t, v, edges, target=1.2, band=0.024, exc=0.096, rec_lim=100e-6):
    """compute max excursion and recovery after each edge"""
    out = []
    for te in edges:
        # samples after edge
        idx = [i for i in range(len(t)) if t[i] >= te]
        if not idx:
            out.append((te, None, None, None)); continue
        seg_t = [t[i] for i in idx]; seg_v = [v[i] for i in idx]
        dev = [abs(x-target) for x in seg_v]
        maxdev = max(dev)
        # recovery: last entry into band that remains
        rec = None
        for j in range(len(idx)):
            if abs(seg_v[j]-target) <= band and all(abs(seg_v[k]-target) <= band for k in range(j, len(idx))):
                rec = seg_t[j]-te
                break
        out.append((te, maxdev, rec, dev[-1]))
    return out

if __name__ == "__main__":
    corner = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "N"
    cl = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 10e-9
    for (lo, hi, nm) in [(1e-6, 100e-6, "light_heavy"), (20e-6, 100e-6, "mid_heavy")]:
        r = tran_profile(lo, hi, cl, f"tran_{nm}_{corner}", corner)
        if r is None: continue
        (t, v), edges = r
        print(f"== {nm} cl={cl} corner={corner}: Vout(0)={v[0]:.5f}")
        res = analyze(t, v, [e for e in edges[1:]], )
        for te, md, rec, last in res:
            rs = f"{rec*1e6:.1f}us" if rec is not None else "NEVER"
            print(f"   edge@{te*1e6:.0f}us maxdev={md*1e3:.2f}mV recovery={rs} final={last*1e3:.2f}mV")

import re
for name in ['n_tt','n_ss','n_ff','p_tt','p_ss','p_ff']:
    txt=open(f'/workspace/work/sky130/{name}.pm3.spice').read()
    # split models
    blocks = txt.split('.model ')
    for b in blocks[1:]:
        m = re.search(r'lmin = ([\d.e+-]+) lmax = ([\d.e+-]+) wmin = ([\d.e+-]+) wmax = ([\d.e+-]+)', b)
        if not m: continue
        lmin,lmax,wmin,wmax = [float(x) for x in m.groups()]
        if abs(lmin-1.5e-7)<1e-9 and wmin==7e-6:
            v = re.search(r'\n\+ vth0 = ([\d.e+-]+)', b)
            k1 = re.search(r'\n\+ k1 = ([\d.e+-]+)', b)
            ua = re.search(r'\n\+ ua = ([\d.e+-]+)', b)
            print(name, 'L=',lmin,lmax,'W=',wmin,wmax, 'vth0=', v.group(1) if v else '?', 'k1=',k1.group(1) if k1 else '?', 'ua=',ua.group(1) if ua else '?')
            break

/workspace/work/prep_models.py
content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Prepare self-contained per-corner model include files from skywater-pdk sources."""
import re, os
MDIR = "/workspace/work/models"

# missing params referenced by pfet base pm3 (LOD/WPE mismatch) -> 0 for nominal sims
MISSING = ["wlod_diff","kvth0_diff","lkvth0_diff","wkvth0_diff","ku0_diff",
           "lku0_diff","wku0_diff","kvsat_diff","toxe_slope1","vth0_slope1",
           "voff_slope1","nfactor_slope1","toxe_slope","vth0_slope","voff_slope",
           "nfactor_slope","toxe_slope2","vth0_slope2","voff_slope2","nfactor_slope2"]

def fix_includes(path):
    txt = open(path).read()
    def rep(m):
        fn = m.group(1)
        return f'.include {MDIR}/{os.path.basename(fn)}'
    return re.sub(r'\.include\s+"([^"]+)"', rep, txt)

for c in ("tt","ss","ff"):
    # nfet corner file (already contains bin params, includes corner-specific pm3)
    n_src = f"{MDIR}/n_{c}.corner.spice"
    n_out = f"{MDIR}/n_{c}_fixed.inc"
    open(n_out,"w").write(fix_includes(n_src))
    # pfet discrete corner file (bin params, includes base pm3)
    p_src = f"{MDIR}/p_{c}.corner.spice"
    p_out = f"{MDIR}/p_{c}_fixed.inc"
    open(p_out,"w").write(fix_includes(p_src))
    # header with extra params
    hdr = f"{MDIR}/extra_{c}.inc"
    with open(hdr,"w") as f:
        for p in MISSING:
            f.write(f".param sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n")
            f.write(f".param sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n")
    print("wrote", n_out, p_out, hdr)
RESULT

import re, os
MD="/workspace/work/models"
used=set(); defined=set()
for f in os.listdir(MD):
    t=open(os.path.join(MD,f)).read()
    if f.endswith('.pm3.spice'):
        used |= set(re.findall(r'sky130_fd_pr__\w+', t))
    if 'corner' in f and 'fixed' not in f:
        defined |= set(re.findall(r'\.param\s+(sky130_fd_pr__\w+)', t))
        defined |= set(re.findall(r'^\+\s+(sky130_fd_pr__\w+)\s*=', t, re.M))
extra=set()
for f in ['extra_tt.inc']:
    t=open(os.path.join(MD,f)).read()
    extra |= set(re.findall(r'\.param\s+(sky130_fd_pr__\w+)', t))
missing = sorted((used - defined - extra))
for m in missing: print(m)
print("total", len(missing))
EOF

shell
INPgetModBin(CKTcircuit *ckt, char *name, INPmodel **model, INPtables *tab, char *line)
{
    INPmodel    *modtmp;
    double       l, w, lmin, lmax, wmin, wmax;
    double       parse_values[4];
    bool         parse_found[4];
    static char *instance_tokens[] = { "l", "w", "nf", "wnflag" };
    static char *model_tokens[]    = { "lmin", "lmax", "wmin", "wmax" };
    double       scale;
    int          wnflag;

    if (!cp_getvar("scale", CP_REAL, &scale, 0))
        scale = 1;

    if (!cp_getvar("wnflag", CP_NUM, &wnflag, 0)) {
        if (newcompat.spe || newcompat.hs)
            wnflag = 1;
        else
            wnflag = 0;
    }

    *model = NULL;

    /* read W and L. If not on the instance line, leave */
    if (!parse_line(line, instance_tokens, 2, parse_values, parse_found))
        return NULL;

    /* This is for reading nf. If nf is not available, set to 1 if in HSPICE or Spectre compatibility mode */
    if (!parse_line(line, instance_tokens, 3, parse_values, parse_found)) {
        parse_values[2] = 1.; /* divisor */
    }
    /* This is for reading wnflag from instance. If it is not available, no change.
       If instance wnflag == 0, set divisor to 1, else use instance nf */
    else if (parse_line(line, instance_tokens, 4, parse_values, parse_found)) {
        /* wnflag from instance overrules: no use of nf */
        if (parse_values[3] == 0) {
            parse_values[2] = 1.; /* divisor */
        }
    }
    /* We do have nf, but no wnflag on the instance. Now it depends on the default
       wnflag or on the .options wnflag */
    else {
        if (wnflag == 0)
            parse_values[2] = 1.; /* divisor */
    }


    l = parse_values[0] * scale;
    w = parse_values[1] / parse_values[2] * scale;

    for (modtmp = modtab; modtmp; modtmp = modtmp->INPnextModel) {

        if (model_name_match(name, modtmp->INPmodName) < 2)
            continue;

        /* skip if not binnable */
        if (modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM3") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM3v32") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM3v0") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM3v1") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM4") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM4v5") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM4v6") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("BSIM4v7") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("HiSIM2") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("HiSIMHV1") &&
            modtmp->INPmodType != INPtypelook("HiSIMHV2"))
        {
            continue;
        }

        /* if illegal device type */
        if (modtmp->INPmodType < 0) {
            *model = NULL;
            return tprintf("Unknown device type for model %s\n", name);
        }

        if (!parse_line(modtmp->INPmodLine->line, model_tokens, 4, parse_values, parse_found))
            continue;

        lmin = parse_values[0]; lmax = parse_values[1];
        wmin = parse_values[2]; wmax = parse_values[3];

        if (in_range(l, lmin, lmax) && in_range(w, wmin, wmax)) {
            /* create unless model is already defined */
            if (!modtmp->INPmodfast) {
                int error = create_model(ckt, modtmp, tab);
                if (error)
                    return NULL;
            }

            *model = modtmp;
            return NULL;
        }
    }

    return NULL;
}


char *
INPgetMod(CKTcircuit *ckt, char *name, INPmodel **model, INPtables *tab)
{
    INPmodel *modtmp;

#ifdef TRACE
    printf("In INPgetMod, examining model %s ...\n", name);
#endif

    if (modtabhash) {
        modtmp = nghash_find(modtabhash, name);
        if (modtmp) {
            /* found the model in question - now instantiate if necessary */

python3 - <<'EOF'
# build flat model files: corner params (no includes) + flat model file
import re
MD="/workspace/work/models"
for c in ("tt","ss","ff"):
    # PFET: discrete corner params + flat base pm3
    t = open(f"{MD}/p_{c}.corner.spice").read()
    t = re.sub(r'^\.include.*$','',t,flags=re.M)
    base = open(f"{MD}/pfet_base.pm3.spice").read()
    base = re.sub(r'^\.subckt.*$','* subckt removed',base,flags=re.M)
    base = re.sub(r'^\.ends.*$','* ends removed',base,flags=re.M)
    open(f"{MD}/flat_p_{c}.inc","w").write(t+"\n"+base)
    # NFET: corner file params (no include) + flat corner pm3
    t = open(f"{MD}/n_{c}.corner.spice").read()
    t = re.sub(r'^\.include.*$','',t,flags=re.M)
    base = open(f"{MD}/n_{c}.pm3.spice").read()
    base = re.sub(r'^\.subckt.*$','* subckt removed',base,flags=re.M)
    base = re.sub(r'^\.ends.*$','* ends removed',base,flags=re.M)
    open(f"{MD}/flat_n_{c}.inc","w").write(t+"\n"+base)
print("ok")
EOF

python3 - <<'EOF'
import re
MD="/workspace/work/models"
def extract_models(txt):
    out=[]; keep=False
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith('.model'):
            keep=True; out.append(line); continue
        if keep and (line.startswith('+') or line.strip()==''):
            out.append(line); continue
        if keep and not line.startswith('+'):
            keep=False
    return "\n".join(out)
for c in ("tt","ss","ff"):
    t = re.sub(r'^\.include.*$','',open(f"{MD}/p_{c}.corner.spice").read(),flags=re.M)
    t = t.split('.model')[0]   # only the .param block before the first model
    base = extract_models(open(f"{MD}/pfet_base.pm3.spice").read())
    open(f"{MD}/flat_p_{c}.inc","w").write(t+"\n"+base)
    t = re.sub(r'^\.include.*$','',open(f"{MD}/n_{c}.corner.spice").read(),flags=re.M)
    t = t.split('.model')[0]
    base = extract_models(open(f"{MD}/n_{c}.pm3.spice").read())
    open(f"{MD}/flat_n_{c}.inc","w").write(t+"\n"+base)
print("ok", sum(1 for _ in open(f"{MD}/flat_n_tt.inc")), sum(1 for _ in open(f"{MD}/flat_p_tt.inc")))
EOF

0:24:22
shell
int model_name_match(const char *token, const char *model_name)
{
    const char *p;
    size_t token_len = strlen(token);

    if (strncmp(token, model_name, token_len) != 0)
        return 0;

    p = model_name + token_len;

    // exact match
    if (*p == '\0')
        return 1;

    // check for .
    if (*p++ != '.')
        return 0;

    // minimum one trailing char
    if (*p == '\0')
        return 0;

    // all of them digits
    for (; *p; p++)
        if (!isdigit_c(*p))
            return 0;

    return 2;
} /* end of funtion model_name_match */

content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build flat per-corner model files where each device geometry gets its own
explicitly-named model card (avoids ngspice binning lookup quirks)."""
import re, os, sys

MD = "/workspace/work/models"

def parse_bins(pm3file):
    """return list of (name, lmin,lmax,wmin,wmax, cardtext)"""
    txt = open(pm3file).read().splitlines()
    bins = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(txt):
        line = txt[i]
        if line.startswith('.model'):
            card = [line]
            j = i + 1
            while j < len(txt) and (txt[j].startswith('+') or txt[j].startswith('*')):
                card.append(txt[j]); j += 1
            body = "\n".join(card)
            m = re.search(r'lmin\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+lmax\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+wmin\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+wmax\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)', body)
            if m:
                bins.append((line.split()[1], *[float(x) for x in m.groups()], body))
            i = j
        else:
            i += 1
    return bins

def param_block(f):
    txt = open(f).read()
    out = []
    for line in txt.splitlines():
        if line.startswith('.model'):
            break
        if line.startswith('.include'):
            continue
        out.append(line)
    return "\n".join(out)

def build(corner, geom_n, geom_p, outfile):
    """geom_n/geom_p: list of (W_um, L_um, modelname)"""
    parts = []
    parts.append("".join(f".param sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n.param sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n"
        for p in ["wlod_diff","kvth0_diff","lkvth0_diff","wkvth0_diff","ku0_diff","lku0_diff",
                  "wku0_diff","kvsat_diff","toxe_slope1","vth0_slope1","voff_slope1","nfactor_slope1",
                  "toxe_slope","vth0_slope","voff_slope","nfactor_slope","toxe_slope2","vth0_slope2",
                  "voff_slope2","nfactor_slope2","toxe_slope_spectre","vth0_slope_spectre",
                  "voff_slope_spectre","nfactor_slope_spectre","dlc_rotweak","lint_diff","wint_diff",
                  "dlc_diff","dwc_diff"]) )
    parts.append(param_block(f"{MD}/n_{corner}.corner.spice"))
    parts.append(param_block(f"{MD}/p_{corner}.corner.spice"))
    nb = parse_bins(f"{MD}/n_{corner}.pm3.spice")
    pb = parse_bins(f"{MD}/pfet_base.pm3.spice")
    for W, L, name in geom_n:
        Wm, Lm = W*1e-6, L*1e-6
        cand = [b for b in nb if b[1] <= Lm <= b[2] and b[3] <= Wm <= b[4]]
        assert len(cand) == 1, (name, W, L, [c[0] for c in cand])
        card = re.sub(r'^\.model\s+\S+', f".model {name}", cand[0][5], count=1, flags=re.M)
        parts.append(f"* NFET W={W} L={L} bin {cand[0][0]}\n" + card)
    for W, L, name in geom_p:
        Wm, Lm = W*1e-6, L*1e-6
        cand = [b for b in pb if b[1] <= Lm <= b[2] and b[3] <= Wm <= b[4]]
        assert len(cand) == 1, (name, W, L, [c[0] for c in cand])
        card = re.sub(r'^\.model\s+\S+', f".model {name}", cand[0][5], count=1, flags=re.M)
        parts.append(f"* PFET W={W} L={L} bin {cand[0][0]}\n" + card)
    open(outfile, "w").write("\n".join(parts) + "\n")
    return outfile

if __name__ == "__main__":
    geoms_n = [(90,0.15,"n_90_015"), (45,0.3,"n_45_030"), (20,0.15,"n_20_015"),
               (40,2,"n_40_2"), (8,1,"n_8_1"), (8,2,"n_8_2")]
    geoms_p = [(60,0.15,"p_60_015"), (45,0.15,"p_45_015"), (20,0.15,"p_20_015"),
               (8,1,"p_8_1"), (20,4,"p_20_4"), (20,2,"p_20_2")]
    for c in ("tt","ss","ff"):
        build(c, geoms_n, geoms_p, f"{MD}/mdl_{c}.inc")
    print("built")

content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build flat per-corner model files: one explicit .model card per device geometry."""
import re, os, sys

MD = "/workspace/work/models"

EXTRA = ["wlod_diff","kvth0_diff","lkvth0_diff","wkvth0_diff","ku0_diff","lku0_diff",
         "wku0_diff","kvsat_diff","toxe_slope1","vth0_slope1","voff_slope1","nfactor_slope1",
         "toxe_slope","vth0_slope","voff_slope","nfactor_slope","toxe_slope2","vth0_slope2",
         "voff_slope2","nfactor_slope2","toxe_slope_spectre","vth0_slope_spectre",
         "voff_slope_spectre","nfactor_slope_spectre","dlc_rotweak","lint_diff","wint_diff",
         "dlc_diff","dwc_diff","rshp_mult","toxe_mult","overlap_mult","ajunction_mult",
         "pjunction_mult","rshn_mult"]

def parse_bins(pm3file):
    txt = open(pm3file).read().splitlines()
    bins = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(txt):
        line = txt[i]
        if line.startswith('.model'):
            card = [line]; j = i + 1
            while j < len(txt) and (txt[j].startswith('+') or txt[j].startswith('*')):
                card.append(txt[j]); j += 1
            body = "\n".join(card)
            m = re.search(r'lmin\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+lmax\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+wmin\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)\s+wmax\s*=\s*([\d.eE+-]+)', body)
            if m:
                bins.append((line.split()[1], *[float(x) for x in m.groups()], body))
            i = j
        else:
            i += 1
    return bins

def param_block(f):
    out = []
    for line in open(f).read().splitlines():
        if line.startswith('.model'):
            break
        if line.startswith('.include'):
            continue
        out.append(line)
    return "\n".join(out)

def pick(bins, W_um, L_um, name):
    Wm, Lm = W_um*1e-6, L_um*1e-6
    cand = [b for b in bins if b[1] <= Lm <= b[2] and b[3] <= Wm <= b[4]]
    if not cand:
        raise SystemExit(f"no bin for W={W_um} L={L_um} ({name})")
    b = cand[0]
    return re.sub(r'^\.model\s+\S+', f".model {name}", b[5], count=1, flags=re.M), b[0]

def build(corner, geoms, outfile):
    parts = []
    parts.append("".join(f".param sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n.param sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8__{p} = 0\n" for p in EXTRA))
    parts.append(param_block(f"{MD}/n_{corner}.corner.spice"))
    parts.append(param_block(f"{MD}/p_{corner}.corner.spice"))
    nbin = parse_bins(f"{MD}/n_{corner}.pm3.spice")
    pbin = parse_bins(f"{MD}/p_{corner}.pm3.spice")
    for pol, W, L, name in geoms:
        bins = nbin if pol == 'n' else pbin
        card, bname = pick(bins, W, L, name)
        parts.append(f"* {pol} W={W} L={L} src-bin {bname}\n" + card)
    open(outfile, "w").write("\n".join(parts) + "\n")
    return outfile

GEOMS = [
    ('n', 90, 0.15, 'n_90_015'), ('n', 45, 0.3, 'n_45_030'), ('n', 100, 0.3, 'n_100_030'),
    ('n', 20, 0.15, 'n_20_015'), ('n', 40, 2, 'n_40_2'), ('n', 8, 1, 'n_8_1'),

]

if __name__ == "__main__":

content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Local LDO testbench harness: flattens X-devices to M-devices with explicit models."""
import os, re, subprocess, sys

WORK = "/workspace/work"
MD = f"{WORK}/models"
NG = f"{WORK}/ng/src/ngspice"

CORNERS = {
    "N":   ("tt", 1.6, 27, 1e-6),
    "SLC": ("ss", 1.4, -20, 1e-6),
    "SLH": ("ss", 1.4, 85, 1e-6),
    "SHC": ("ss", 1.8, -20, 1e-6),
    "SHH": ("ss", 1.8, 85, 1e-6),
    "FLC": ("ff", 1.4, -20, 1e-6),
    "FLH": ("ff", 1.4, 85, 1e-6),
    "FHC": ("ff", 1.8, -20, 1e-6),
    "FHH": ("ff", 1.8, 85, 1e-6),
}

def devname(pol, w, l):
    def f(x):
        s = ("%g" % x)
        return s.replace(".", "p")
    return f"{pol}_{f(w)}_{f(l)}"

def scan_geoms(dut):
    geoms = []
    for line in dut.splitlines():
        s = line.strip()
        if not s.lower().startswith("x"):
            continue
        toks = s.split()
        model = toks[-3] if len(toks) >= 6 else None
        # tokens: name d g s b sub l=.. w=..
        pol = None
        for t in toks:
            if "nfet" in t: pol = "n"
            if "pfet" in t: pol = "p"
        l = w = None
        for t in toks:
            if t.lower().startswith("l="): l = float(t.split("=")[1])
            if t.lower().startswith("w="): w = float(t.split("=")[1])
        if pol and l and w:
            geoms.append((pol, w, l))
    return sorted(set(geoms))

def build_models(dut, corner):
    """Build a model include file with explicit models for each geometry in dut."""
    sys.path.insert(0, WORK)
    import mkmodel
    geoms = [(p, w, l, devname(p, w, l)) for (p, w, l) in scan_geoms(dut)]
    out = f"{MD}/build_{corner}.inc"
    mkmodel.build(corner, geoms, out)
    return out

def flatten(dut):
    """Convert X-device lines to M-device lines with explicit model names."""
    out = []
    for line in dut.splitlines():
        s = line.strip()
        if s.lower().startswith("x") and ("nfet" in s or "pfet" in s):
            toks = s.split()
            name, d, g, ss, b = toks[0], toks[1], toks[2], toks[3], toks[4]
            pol = "n" if "nfet" in s else "p"
            l = w = None
            m = None
            for t in toks:
                tl = t.lower()
                if tl.startswith("l="): l = float(t.split("=")[1])
                if tl.startswith("w="): w = float(t.split("=")[1])
                if tl.startswith("m="): m = int(t.split("=")[1])
            mn = devname(pol, w, l)
            mstr = f" m={m}" if m else ""
            out.append(f"M{name[1:]} {d} {g} {ss} {b} {mn} l={l}e-6 w={w}e-6{mstr}")
        else:
            out.append(line)
    return "\n".join(out)

def run(netlist, tag, timeout=1800):
    os.makedirs(f"{WORK}/out", exist_ok=True)
    f = f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.sp"
    open(f, "w").write(netlist)
    p = subprocess.run([NG, "-b", "-o", f"{WORK}/out/{tag}.log", f],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
    return p.returncode, f

def dc_net(dut, corner, iload, cl):
    c = CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = build_models(dut, c[0])
    flat = flatten(dut)
    return f"""* dc {corner} iload={iload}
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {iload}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
op
wrdata {WORK}/out/dc_{corner}_{iload}.dat v(vout) i(vdd) i(vref)
.endc
.end
"""

def tran_net(dut, corner, cl, prof, tstop, tstep="50n"):
    c = CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = build_models(dut, c[0])
    flat = flatten(dut)
    pwl = " ".join(f"{t:.10g} {i:.10g}" for t, i in prof)
    return f"""* tran {corner}
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 pwl({pwl})
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
tran {tstep} {tstop}
wrdata {WORK}/out/tran_{corner}.dat v(vout) i(vdd)
.endc
.end
"""

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/work')
import harness as H

def dbg(dutfile, corner='N', iload='1u', cl='10n'):
    dut = open(dutfile).read()
    c = H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = H.build_models(dut, c[0])
    flat = H.flatten(dut)
    net = f"""* debug
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {iload}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
op
print v(vout) v(vg) v(n1) v(n2) v(nt) v(n3) v(fb) i(vdd) i(vref)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc, f = H.run(net, 'dbg')
    log = open(f'{H.WORK}/out/dbg.log').read()
    return rc, log

if __name__ == "__main__":
    rc, log = dbg(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else '/workspace/work/dut_v1.spice',
                  sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv)>2 else 'N',
                  sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv)>3 else '1u')
    # print lines containing voltages
    for line in log.splitlines():
        if any(k in line for k in ('v(vout)','v(vg)','v(n1)','v(n2)','v(nt)','v(n3)','v(fb)','i(vdd)','i(vref)')):
            print(line)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Loop-gain (AC) analysis of the LDO."""
import sys, os, math, cmath
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/work')
import harness as H

def run_ac(dutfile, corner, il, cl=10e-9, vout_dc=None):
    dut = open(dutfile).read()
    c = H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = H.build_models(dut, c[0])
    flat = H.flatten(dut)
    vout_dc = vout_dc if vout_dc is not None else 1.2
    net = f"""* ac loop
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {il}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
RF1 vout fbo 2e6
RF2 fbo 0 2e6
Vbrk fbi fbo dc 0 ac 1
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref fbi DUTA
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
ac dec 20 0.1 1e8
set filetype=ascii
wrdata /workspace/work/out/ac2.dat v(fbo)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc, f = H.run(net, f'ac2_{corner}_{il}')
    p = '/workspace/work/out/ac2.dat'
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    data = []
    for l in open(p):
        if l.startswith('#') or not l.strip():
            continue
        cols = [float(x) for x in l.split()]
        # wrdata: freq, re, im
        fq, re, im = cols[0], cols[1], cols[2]
        data.append((fq, complex(re, im)))
    return data

def summarize(data, label=""):
    # T = -v(fbo)
    T = [(f, -c) for f, c in data]
    T0 = abs(T[0][1])
    # find unity gain crossing
    ugf = None
    for i in range(1, len(T)):
        if abs(T[i][1]) < 1.0 <= abs(T[i-1][1]):
            f0, c0 = T[i-1]; f1, c1 = T[i]
            ugf = f0 * (f1/f0)**((math.log10(abs(c0))-0)/(math.log10(abs(c0))-math.log10(abs(c1))))
            ph = math.degrees(cmath.phase(c1))
            pm = 180 + ph if ph < 0 else ph
            return T0, ugf, pm
    return T0, None, None

if __name__ == "__main__":
    for corner in sys.argv[1:] or ['N', 'FLH', 'SLC']:
        for il in ['1u', '100u']:
            d = run_ac('/workspace/work/dut_v2ac.spice', corner, il)
            if d is None:
                print(corner, il, "FAILED"); continue
            T0, ugf, pm = summarize(d)
            print(f"{corner:4s} il={il:6s} T_dc={T0:8.1f} (={20*math.log10(T0):6.1f} dB) UGF={ugf and ugf/1e3:8.1f} kHz PM={pm and round(pm,1)}")

import sys, math, cmath
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace/work')
from loopgain import run_ac, summarize
for corner in ['N','FLH','SLC','FHH']:
    for il in ['1u','100u']:
        d=run_ac('/workspace/work/dut_v2ac.spice', corner, il)
        if d is None: print(corner, il, "FAILED"); continue
        T0,ugf,pm=summarize(d)
        # print magnitude at few freqs

        if ugf: s.append(f"UGF={ugf/1e3:.1f}kHz PM={pm:.1f}")
        else:
            # find max freq where |T|>1
            s.append("no crossing; |T| at 1MHz=%.2f, at 1kHz=%.1f"%(abs(T0), abs(d[0][1])))
        print(corner, il, " ".join(s))

import sys, math, cmath
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace/work')
from loopgain import run_ac
def T_of(d):
    out=[]
    for f,c in d:
        v=c  # v(fbo)
        if abs(1+v)<1e-15: continue
        out.append((f, -v/(1+v)))
    return out
for corner in ['N','FLH','SLC','FHH']:
    for il in ['100u','1u']:
        d=run_ac('/workspace/work/dut_v2ac.spice', corner, il)
        T=T_of(d)
        T0=abs(T[0][1]); print(f"--- {corner} il={il}: T_dc={T0:.0f} ({20*math.log10(T0):.1f}dB)")
        # find UGF
        for i in range(1,len(T)):
            if abs(T[i][1])<1.0<=abs(T[i-1][1]):
                f0,c0=T[i-1]; f1,c1=T[i]
                ugf=f0*(f1/f0)**(math.log10(abs(c0))/(math.log10(abs(c0))-math.log10(abs(c1))))
                ph=math.degrees(cmath.phase(c1))
                print(f"    UGF={ugf/1e3:.1f} kHz  phase={ph:.1f} deg  PM={180+ph:.1f}")
                break
        # print T at decades
        s="    "
        for f,c in T:
            if abs(math.log10(f/1)-round(math.log10(f)))<0.05 and f>=1:
                s+=f"{f:.0e}:{20*math.log10(abs(c)):.0f}dB({math.degrees(cmath.phase(c)):.0f}) "
        print(s)

content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Correct loop-gain extraction (wrdata column layout: f, re, im per vector)."""
import sys, os, math, cmath
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/work')
import harness as H

def make_ac_dut(dut):
    """Return DUT variant with divider removed and FB exposed."""
    lines = []
    for l in dut.splitlines():
        s = l.strip()
        if s.startswith('RF1') or s.startswith('RF2'):
            continue
        if s.startswith('.subckt'):
            s = s.replace('.subckt DUT ', '.subckt DUTA ').rstrip() + ' FBI'
        lines.append(s)
    return "\n".join(lines)

def run(dut, corner, il, cl=10e-9, npts=25):
    duta = make_ac_dut(dut)
    c = H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = H.build_models(duta, c[0])
    flat = H.flatten(duta)
    net = f"""* loop gain
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {il}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
RF1 vout fbo 2e6
RF2 fbo 0 2e6
Vbrk fbi fbo dc 0 ac 1
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref fbi DUTA
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
ac dec {npts} 0.1 1e8
set filetype=ascii
wrdata /workspace/work/out/lg.dat v(fbi) v(fbo)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc, f = H.run(net, 'lg')
    p = '/workspace/work/out/lg.dat'
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    data = []
    for l in open(p):
        if l.startswith('#') or not l.strip():
            continue
        v = [float(x) for x in l.split()]
        if len(v) < 6:
            continue
        fq = v[0]
        vi = complex(v[1], v[2])
        vo = complex(v[4], v[5])
        data.append((fq, vi, vo))
    return data

def loopgain(data):
    """T = -H, H = v(fbo)/v(fbi) computed from the injection solution."""
    T = []
    for f, vi, vo in data:
        if abs(vi) < 1e-15:
            continue
        H_ = vo / vi
        T.append((f, -H_))
    return T

def metrics(dut, corner, il, cl=10e-9):
    d = run(dut, corner, il, cl)
    if d is None:
        return None
    T = loopgain(d)
    if not T:
        return None
    T0 = abs(T[0][1])
    # unity crossing
    ugf = None; pm = None
    for i in range(1, len(T)):
        if abs(T[i][1]) < 1.0 <= abs(T[i-1][1]):
            f0, c0 = T[i-1]; f1, c1 = T[i]
            denom = (math.log10(abs(c0)) - math.log10(abs(c1)))
            if abs(denom) > 1e-12:
                ugf = f0 * (f1/f0)**(math.log10(abs(c0))/denom)
            # interpolate phase
            p0 = math.degrees(cmath.phase(c0)); p1 = math.degrees(cmath.phase(c1))
            if p1 - p0 > 180: p1 -= 360
            if p1 - p0 < -180: p1 += 360
            w = (math.log10(ugf) - math.log10(f0))/(math.log10(f1)-math.log10(f0))
            ph = p0 + w*(p1-p0)
            pm = 180 + ph
            break
    return dict(T0=T0, ugf=ugf, pm=pm, T=T)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    dut = open(sys.argv[1]).read()
    for corner in (sys.argv[2:] or ['N','FLH','SLC','FHH']):
        for il in ['100u','1u']:
            m = metrics(dut, corner, il)
            if m is None:
                print(corner, il, "FAIL"); continue
            s = f"{corner:4s} il={il:5s} T_dc={m['T0']:7.1f} ({20*math.log10(m['T0']):5.1f}dB)"
            if m['ugf']:
                s += f"  UGF={m['ugf']/1e3:8.1f}kHz  PM={m['pm']:5.1f}deg"
            else:
                s += "  (no unity crossing)"
            print(s)
RESULT

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Parameterized DUT generator + quick metrics sweep."""
import sys, os, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/work')
import harness as H
import lg

TPL = """* LDO v{v}
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* pass device PMOS
XPASS VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l=0.15 w={wp}
* feedback divider
RF1 VOUT FB {rf}
RF2 FB VSS {rf}
* error amp: NMOS input pair + PMOS mirror load
XM1 N3 FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l={lp} w={wn}
XM2 VG VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l={lp} w={wn}
XM3 N3 N3 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l={lm} w={wm}
XM4 VG N3 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l={lm} w={wm}
* tail NMOS (mirror ratio {ratio} vs XM6)
XM5 NT NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.5 w={wt}
* bias reference: resistor + NMOS diode
RB1 VIN NB {rb}
XM6 NB NB VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.5 w={wt6}
.ends DUT
"""

def make(**kw):
    d = dict(v=3, wp=60, rf='2e6', lp='0.15', wn=45, lm='0.15', wm=20,
             wt=100, wt6=50, rb='3e6')
    d.update(kw)
    return TPL.format(**d)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import itertools
    variants = {
        'A_base':      dict(),
        'B_mir_L1':    dict(lm='1', wm=20),
        'C_pair_L03':  dict(lp='0.3', wn=90),
        'D_both':      dict(lp='0.3', wn=90, lm='1', wm=20),
        'E_both2':     dict(lp='0.3', wn=90, lm='2', wm=20),
    }
    for name, kw in variants.items():
        dut = make(**kw)
        out = []
        for corner, il in [('N','100u'), ('SLC','100u'), ('FLH','100u'), ('FHH','100u'),
                           ('N','1u'), ('SLC','1u'), ('FHH','1u')]:
            m = lg.metrics(dut, corner, il)
            if m is None:
                out.append(f"{corner}/{il}:FAIL"); continue
            ug = m['ugf']/1e3 if m['ugf'] else float('nan')
            pm = m['pm'] if m['pm'] else float('nan')
            out.append(f"{corner}/{il}:T={m['T0']:.0f},UGF={ug:.0f}k,PM={pm:.0f}")
        print(f"{name:12s} " + " | ".join(out))

content
* LDO v3: NMOS pair EA + PMOS mirror, adaptive bias via pass-current sense
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* pass device PMOS (W/L=400)
XPASS VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l=0.15 w=60
* pass-current sense device (ratio ~1/48)
XSEN N1 VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l=0.15 w=1.26
* feedback divider
RF1 VOUT FB 2e6
RF2 FB VSS 2e6
* error amp: NMOS input pair + PMOS mirror load (out at VG)
XM1 N3 FB NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.25 w=60
XM2 VG VREF NT VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.25 w=60
XM3 N3 N3 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l=1 w=20
XM4 VG N3 VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 l=1 w=20
* tail NMOS (mirror ratio 2 vs XM6)
XM5 NT N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.5 w=100
* bias node: NMOS diode + floor resistor + sense current
XM6 N1 N1 VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 l=0.5 w=50
RB1 VIN N1 3e6
.ends DUT

import lg
dut=open('dut_v3.spice').read()
for corner,il in [('N','100u'),('FLH','100u')]:

    T=lg.loopgain(d)
    print(f"=== {corner}/{il}")

    for f,c in T:
        m=abs(c)
        if prev is None or (m<prev and f>1e2) or f<1e3:
            pass
        if f in [1.0] or True:
            pass

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Full validation: DC across corners/loads, overhead, transient profiles."""
import sys, os, math
sys.path.insert(0, '/workspace/work')
import harness as H

def dc_run(dut, corner, il, cl=10e-9):
    c = H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl = H.build_models(dut, c[0])
    flat = H.flatten(dut)
    net = f"""* dcv
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {il}
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
op
set filetype=ascii
wrdata {H.WORK}/out/dcv.dat v(vout) i(vdd) i(vref)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc, f = H.run(net, 'dcv')
    p = f'{H.WORK}/out/dcv.dat'
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    v = [float(x) for x in open(p).read().split()]
    # cols: freq, re, im... : v(vout)=v[1], i(vdd)=v[4], i(vref)=v[7]
    return dict(vout=v[1], ivdd=-v[4], ivref=v[7])

def dc_table(dut, loads=('1u','20u','100u'), corners=None, cl=10e-9):
    corners = corners or list(H.CORNERS)
    print(f"{'corner':6s}" + "".join(f"{('V@'+l):>12s}" for l in loads) + f"{'Iq@1u(uA)':>12s}{'Iq@100u(uA)':>13s}")
    ok = True
    for corner in corners:
        row = []
        for il in loads:
            r = dc_run(dut, corner, il, cl)
            if r is None:
                row.append(None); continue
            row.append(r)
        s = f"{corner:6s}"
        for i, r in enumerate(row):
            if r is None:
                s += f"{'FAIL':>12s}"; ok = False
            else:
                s += f"{r['vout']:12.5f}"
        if row[0]: s += f"{row[0]['ivdd']*1e6:12.3f}"
        if row[2]: s += f"{row[2]['ivdd']*1e6:13.3f}"
        print(s)
    return ok

if __name__ == "__main__":
    dut = open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else '/workspace/work/dut_v3.spice').read()
    cl = float(sys.argv[2]) if len(sys.argv) > 2 else 10e-9
    dc_table(dut, cl=cl)

cd /workspace/work && python3 - <<'EOF'
import sys, os, math
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace/work')
import harness as H, lg
def inspect(dutfile, corner, il):
    dut=open(dutfile).read()
    duta=lg.make_ac_dut(dut)
    c=H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl=H.build_models(duta,c[0]); flat=H.flatten(duta)
    net=f"""* inspect
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 dc {il}
Cload vout 0 10n
RF1 vout fbo 2e6
RF2 fbo 0 2e6
Vbrk fbi fbo dc 0 ac 1
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref fbi DUTA
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
ac dec 4 0.1 1e6
set filetype=ascii
wrdata /workspace/work/out/insp.dat v(fbi) v(fbo) v(xdut.vg) v(vout) v(xdut.n3) v(xdut.nt)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc,f=H.run(net,'insp')
    p='/workspace/work/out/insp.dat'
    rows=[]
    for l in open(p):
        if l.startswith('#') or not l.strip(): continue
        v=[float(x) for x in l.split()]
        rows.append((v[0], complex(v[1],v[2]), complex(v[4],v[5]), complex(v[7],v[8]), complex(v[10],v[11]), complex(v[13],v[14]), complex(v[16],v[17])))
    f,vi,vo,vg,vu,n3,nt=rows[0]
    H_=vo/vi; T=-H_
    print(f"{corner}/{il}: T={abs(T):.1f} A_ea=|vg/fbi|={abs(vg/vi):.2f} passgain=|vout/vg|={abs(vu/vg):.2f} beta=|fbo/vout|={abs(vo/vu):.3f}")
for corner,il in [('FLH','1u'),('N','1u'),('SLC','1u'),('N','100u'),('FLH','100u')]:
    inspect('dut_v4.spice',corner,il)
EOF

cd /workspace/work && python3 - <<'EOF'
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace/work')
import harness as H
def tran(dutfile, corner, cl, prof, tstop, tag):
    dut=open(dutfile).read()
    c=H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl=H.build_models(dut,c[0]); flat=H.flatten(dut)
    pwl=" ".join(f"{t:.10g} {i:.10g}" for t,i in prof)
    net=f"""* tran
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 pwl({pwl})
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
tran 50n {tstop}
set filetype=ascii
wrdata /workspace/work/out/tr_{tag}.dat v(vout)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc,f=H.run(net,f'tr_{tag}')
    p=f'/workspace/work/out/tr_{tag}.dat'
    if not os.path.exists(p): return None
    t=[];v=[]
    for l in open(p):
        if l.startswith('#') or not l.strip(): continue
        c2=[float(x) for x in l.split()]
        t.append(c2[0]); v.append(c2[1])
    return t,v
def prof_lh(lo,hi,edge=2e-6,hold=100e-6,plat=200e-6,tail=100e-6):
    ts=[0,hold,hold+edge,hold+edge+plat,hold+edge+plat+edge,hold+edge+plat+edge+tail]
    is_=[lo,lo,hi,hi,lo,lo]
    return list(zip(ts,is_)), ts[-1]+5e-6, [ts[1],ts[3],ts[4]]
for corner in ['N','FHH']:
    p,ts,edges=prof_lh(1e-6,100e-6)
    r=tran('dut_v6.spice',corner,10e-9,p,ts,f'v6_{corner}')
    if r is None: print(corner,"TRAN FAIL"); continue
    t,v=r
    print(f"--- {corner}: V0={v[0]:.5f} Vend={v[-1]:.5f}")
    for te in edges:
        seg=[(tt,vv) for tt,vv in zip(t,v) if tt>=te]
        if not seg: continue
        mx=max(abs(vv-1.2) for tt,vv in seg)
        # recovery: last entry into +-24mV staying
        rec=None
        for i in range(len(seg)):
            if all(abs(seg[j][1]-1.2)<=0.024 for j in range(i,len(seg))):
                rec=seg[i][0]-te; break
        print(f"   edge@{te*1e6:.0f}us: maxdev={mx*1e3:6.2f}mV  recovery={('%.1fus'%(rec*1e6)) if rec is not None else 'NEVER'}")
EOF

cd /workspace/work && python3 - <<'EOF'
import sys, os
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace/work')
import harness as H
def tran(dutfile, corner, cl, prof, tstop, tag):
    dut=open(dutfile).read()
    c=H.CORNERS[corner]
    mdl=H.build_models(dut,c[0]); flat=H.flatten(dut)
    pwl=" ".join(f"{t:.10g} {i:.10g}" for t,i in prof)
    net=f"""* tran
.include {mdl}
.temp {c[2]}
Vdd vdd 0 {c[1]}
Vref vref 0 0.6
Iload vout 0 pwl({pwl})
Cload vout 0 {cl}
Xdut vdd vout 0 vref DUT
{flat[flat.index('.subckt'):]}
.control
tran 50n {tstop}
set filetype=ascii
wrdata /workspace/work/out/tr_{tag}.dat v(vout)
.endc
.end
"""
    rc,f=H.run(net,f'tr_{tag}')
    p=f'/workspace/work/out/tr_{tag}.dat'
    if not os.path.exists(p): return None
    t=[];v=[]
    for l in open(p):
        if l.startswith('#') or not l.strip(): continue
        c2=[float(x) for x in l.split()]
        t.append(c2[0]); v.append(c2[1])
    return t,v
def prof_lh(lo,hi,edge=2e-6,hold=100e-6,plat=200e-6,tail=100e-6):
    ts=[0,hold,hold+edge,hold+edge+plat,hold+edge+plat+edge,hold+edge+plat+edge+tail]
    return list(zip(ts,[lo,lo,hi,hi,lo,lo])), ts[-1]+5e-6, [ts[1],ts[3]+edge,ts[4]]
def burst(lo,hi,edge=2e-6,hold=100e-6,hi_t=20e-6,lo_t=20e-6,n=8,tail=200e-6):
    ts=[0,hold]; is_=[lo,lo]
    t=hold
    for i in range(n):
        t+=edge; ts.append(t); is_.append(hi)
        t+=hi_t; ts.append(t); is_.append(hi)
        t+=edge; ts.append(t); is_.append(lo)
        t+=lo_t; ts.append(t); is_.append(lo)
    t+=tail; ts.append(t); is_.append(lo)
    return list(zip(ts,is_)), t+5e-6, [hold+edge]
for corner in ['N','FHH','FLH']:
    for cl in [10e-9,100e-9]:
        p,ts,edges=prof_lh(1e-6,100e-6)
        r=tran('dut_v9.spice',corner,cl,p,ts,f'v9_{corner}_{int(cl*1e9)}n')
        if r is None: print(corner,cl,"FAIL"); continue
        t,v=r
        worst=0; recmax=0
        for te in edges:
            seg=[(tt,vv) for tt,vv in zip(t,v) if tt>=te]
            if not seg: continue
            mx=max(abs(vv-1.2) for tt,vv in seg)
            rec=None
            for i in range(len(seg)):
                if all(abs(seg[j][1]-1.2)<=0.024 for j in range(i,len(seg))):
                    rec=seg[i][0]-te; break
            worst=max(worst,mx); recmax=max(recmax, rec if rec is not None else 9.9)
        print(f"{corner} cl={cl*1e9:.0f}nF light_heavy: maxdev={worst*1e3:6.2f}mV recovery={recmax*1e6 if recmax<9 else -1:7.1f}us")
    p,ts,edges=burst(1e-6,100e-6)
    r=tran('dut_v9.spice',corner,10e-9,p,ts,f'v9b_{corner}')
    if r:
        t,v=r
        seg=[(tt,vv) for tt,vv in zip(t,v) if tt>=hold and True]
        mx=max(abs(vv-1.2) for tt,vv in seg)
        print(f"   {corner} burst: maxdev={mx*1e3:.2f}mV")
EOF

cd /workspace && atb-client submit --rationale "Always-on LDO in sky130 (nfet_01v8/pfet_01v8 only). Topology: PMOS common-source pass device (W=100u, L=0.15u; W/L=667 so the 100uA load is delivered at VIN=1.4V with Vov<200mV dropout), feedback divider RF1=1.942M/RF2=2.0M (beta~0.49, ratio trimmed slightly below 1:1 to center the residual systematic input offset inside the +/-2% band), and a single-stage 5T error amp: NMOS input pair (W=60u, L=0.25u, W/L=240) with a PMOS mirror load (W=20u, L=1u). NMOS pair was chosen so the EA output can swing up to VIN and drive the PMOS pass gate over the whole VIN=1.4-1.8V range; the large W/L keeps the pair near weak inversion (stable gm/gain vs corner, and enough headroom at the 0.6V input CM even at SS/-20C), while the long-L PMOS mirror raises EA gain/output resistance without adding offset. Bias: 4Mohm resistor from VIN into an NMOS diode (~0.25uA), mirrored x2 into the tail (~0.5uA) so total quiescent is ~0.9-1.4uA (light-load budget 2uA) and the VREF pin draws only gate leakage (0 A measured). Compensation: no added capacitors (0 F explicit); the dominant pole is the external load cap (10-100nF) at the output, and the internal gate pole (Rout_EA*Cgs_pass ~ hundreds of kHz to MHz) is pushed well above the crossover by keeping EA output resistance low (short-L devices) and loop gain moderate (~40dB), which yields >=60-90deg phase margin, small load-step excursions and immediate band re-entry. All internal nodes reach a unique operating point (no start-up state needed). Measured through the client: nominal group at N, SLC and FHH with CL=10nF - all 18 cases OK: DC VOUT 1.1836-1.2118V (band 1.176-1.224V), max transient droop 23.3mV / overshoot 11.8mV (limit 96mV), recovery 0s (<100us), overhead 0.9-1.4uA (light) and 0.9-1.4uA (heavy), VREF current 0A, KCL residual <1e-15." 2>&1 | tail -15

Summary

Task: Design a continuous-time always-on LDO in sky130 (DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF), 1.2 V out from 1.4–1.8 V in, 1–100 µA loads, 10/100 nF external caps, ≤2 µA quiescent at light load, across 9 PVT corners.

Approach: I built a free local iteration loop (compiled ngspice from source; extracted the sky130 nfet_01v8/pfet_01v8 binned models from the SkyWater PDK and flattened the netlist to M-devices) and validated DC, loop gain and transients locally before spending model time on the client.

Final circuit (/workspace/dut.spice):

  • Pass device: PMOS, W=100 µm/L=0.15 µm (W/L=667) so 100 µA at VIN=1.4 V stays in saturation with <200 mV dropout.
  • Error amp: single-stage 5T OTA, NMOS input pair (L=0.25 µm, W/L=240 — works at the 0.6 V input CM even at SS/−20 °C and lets the output swing to VIN to drive the PMOS gate), PMOS mirror load with L=1 µm (raises gain/output resistance without degrading offset).
  • Compensation: purely parasitic — dominant pole from the external load cap; EA output resistance kept low (short L) so the gate pole sits far above crossover; ~40 dB loop gain, ≥60–90° PM.
  • Bias: 4 MΩ resistor + NMOS diode, mirrored ×2 into the tail (~0.5 µA total), giving 0.9–1.4 µA quiescent and essentially zero VREF current.
  • Divider: 1.942 MΩ/2.0 MΩ, ratio trimmed slightly to center the residual systematic drain-mismatch offset in the ±2 % band.

Client-verified results: all 18 measured cases (nominal groups at N, SLC, FHH with CL=10 nF) passed — DC VOUT 1.1836–1.2118 V, worst transient droop 23.3 mV / overshoot 11.8 mV, recovery 0 s, overhead ≤1.4 µA, KCL residual <1 fA.
